{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_12","type":"agent_message","text":"The paper’s greedy step is a convex-hull feasibility test, not the placeholder’s “stop when the y-span exceeds ε” rule. I’m implementing the streaming equivalent by maintaining the feasible polygon of line parameters for the current segment; when the next point makes that polygon empty, the prior segment is emitted and the point is retried. This preserves the greedy minimum-segment partition and supports arbitrary sorted numeric keys."}}

1;\n        RandomFunction uniform = std::bind(std::uniform_int_distribution<T>(value_min, value_max), engine);\n        std::generate(data.begin(), data.end(), rand);\n    } else {\n        T min = 0;\n        if constexpr (std::is_signed_v<T>)\n            min = -10000;\n        auto value_min = std::numeric_limits<T>::min();\n        auto value_max = std::numeric_limits<T>::max() - 1;\n        RandomFunction uniform_dense = std::bind(std::uniform_int_distribution<T>(min, 10000), engine);\n        RandomFunction uniform_sparse = std::bind(std::uniform_int_distribution<T>(value_min, value_max), engine);\n        RandomFunction binomial = std::bind(std::binomial_distribution<T>(50000), engine);\n        RandomFunction geometric = std::bind(std::geometric_distribution<T>(0.8), engine);\n        auto rand = GENERATE_COPY(as<RandomFunction>{}, uniform_dense, uniform_sparse, binomial, geometric);\n        std::generate(data.begin(), data.end(), rand);\n    }\n\n    std::sort(data.begin(), data.end());\n    while (data.back() == std::numeric_limits<T>::max())\n        data.pop_back();\n    return data;\n}\n\ntemplate<typename... I>\nconstexpr auto add(const std::tuple<T1...> &t1, const std::tuple<T2...> &t2, std::index_sequence<I...>) {\n    return std::tuple{std::get<I>(t1) + std::get<I>(t2)...}; // credits: https://stackoverflow.com/a/50815143\n}\n\ntemplate<typename...
